Airtel Africa plc
(‘Airtel Africa’, or the ‘Company’)
Transaction in Own Shares
London and Lagos, 17 November 2026: Airtel Africa announces that in the period 9
November 2026 to 13 November 2026 it purchased the following number of its ordinary shares
of USD 0.50 each from Citigroup Global Markets Limited (“Citi”) pursuant to the authority
granted by its shareholders as part of its share buyback programme, details of which were
announced on 5 November 2026.
Aggregate information:

9 November 2026 275,000 275.00p 287.00p 282.54p
10 November 2026 185,050 285.80p 293.20p 289.21p
11 November 2026 150,000 283.80p 289.60p 286.09p
12 November 2026 1,089,356 279.40p 291.40p 285.84p
13 November 2026 897,359 279.00p 286.80p 285.00p
The purchased ordinary shares will be cancelled.
Since the commencement of the buyback programme with Citi was announced on 5 November
2026, the Company has purchased 3,396,690 ordinary shares in aggregate, at a volume
weighted average price of 284.68 GBp per ordinary share.

About Airtel Africa
Airtel Africa is a leading provider of telecommunications and mobile money services, with
operations in 14 countries in sub-Saharan Africa. Airtel Africa provides an integrated offer to its
subscribers, including mobile voice and data services as well as mobile money services both
nationally and internationally. The company’s strategy is focused on providing a great customer
experience across the entire footprint, enabling our corporate purpose of transforming lives
across Africa.
www.airtel.africa
